# OECD.AI Policy Navigator

- 큐레이터: OECD.AI / GPAI
- 출처: [Policy Navigator](https://oecd.ai/en/dashboards/policy-initiatives)
- API: `https://api.oecdai.org/policy-initiatives`
- 데이터: `items.json` · `items.csv`

- 건수: **2508건**
- 기간: 2000-10-31 ~ 2026-09-08
